          <Name>open-data-platform:sg_geology_age</Name>
          <Title>Age (Representative)</Title>
          <Abstract>The color and age values have been picked from the 2009/2010 version of the IUGS International stratigraphic chart  (International Commission on Stratigraphy : http://www.stratigraphy.org/). The chart is located at http://www.stratigraphy.org/ics%20chart/09_2010/StratChart2010.pdf. The RGB color has been calculated from the CMYK by using R =  (100 - C) * 2.55 - K * 2.55, G =  (100 - M) * 2.55 - K * 2.55 and B =  (100 - Y) * 2.55 - K * 2.55.  Depending of the device  (plotter versus screen), the colors might not be exactly the same.</Abstract>
